Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any biotech module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 603. The projective resolution has length at most 205 with Betti numbers bounded by 4.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_603(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 603. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^205(S, F) is trivial. QED.
